## `smartjudge_module.py`

### 1. Add PII mask/unmask utilities (missing — new code)
Insert this new block right after the `_ASCII_TO_FA = str.maketrans(...)` line (before `_normalize_fa`):

```python
_PII_PATTERNS = [
    ("IBAN", r'\bIR\d{24}\b'),
    ("MOBILE", r'(?<!\d)09\d{9}(?!\d)'),
    ("NID", r'(?<!\d)\d{10}(?!\d)'),
]


def mask_pii_entities(text: str) -> Tuple[str, Dict[str, str]]:
    """ماسک کردن کد ملی، موبایل و شبا پیش از ارسال متن کاربر به LLM شخص ثالث."""
    if not text:
        return text or "", {}
    mapping: Dict[str, str] = {}
    counters: Dict[str, int] = {}
    for label, pattern in _PII_PATTERNS:
        def _sub(m, label=label):
            counters[label] = counters.get(label, 0) + 1
            token = f"⟦PII_{label}_{counters[label]}⟧"
            mapping[token] = m.group(0)
            return token
        text = re.sub(pattern, _sub, text)
    return text, mapping


def unmask_pii_entities(text: str, mapping: Dict[str, str]) -> str:
    """بازگرداندن مقادیر واقعی PII در خروجی LLM."""
    if not text or not mapping:
        return text
    for token, original in mapping.items():
        text = text.replace(token, original)
    return text
```

Wire it in three places:

**a) `_llm_analyze`** — right after `is_informational = (query_type == QUERY_RESEARCH)`, add:

Use `_retrial_build_user_prompt(masked_case, masked_verdict)` in the `_chat_completion` call, and right after it returns add `content = unmask_pii_entities(content, pii_map)`.

**c) `synthesize_case` endpoint** — before building `system_prompt`/`ctx`, add:

    (re.compile(r'\b[\w\.-]+@[\w\.-]+\.\w+\b'), '<EMAIL>'),
    (re.compile(r'\b\d{10}\b'), '<NATIONAL_ID>'),  # example

]

    masked_text = text

            if original not in mapping:

```python
import re
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple

# Model tiers - exactly 2 models per tier as required
TIER_MODELS: Dict[str, List[str]] = {
    "tier1": ["google/gemini-3.5-flash-lite", "deepseek/deepseek-v4-flash-0731"],
    "tier2": ["openai/gpt-5.6-luna", "deepseek/deepseek-v4-pro"],
    "tier3": ["openai/gpt-5.6-luna", "deepseek/deepseek-v4-pro"],
}

# PII patterns for masking (phone, email, national ID, etc.)
_PII_PATTERNS = [
    (re.compile(r'\b09\d{9}\b'), '<PHONE>'),          # Iranian mobile numbers
    (re.compile(r'\b\d{10}\b'), '<NATIONAL_ID>'),     # Iranian national ID
    (re.compile(r'\b[\w\.-]+@[\w\.-]+\.\w+\b'), '<EMAIL>'),
    (re.compile(r'\b\d{3}-\d{3}-\d{4}\b'), '<PHONE>'), # US-style phone
]

def mask_pii_entities(text: str) -> Tuple[str, Dict[str, str]]:
    """
    Replace PII in the input text with placeholders.
    Returns the masked text and a mapping of original -> placeholder.
    """
    mapping: Dict[str, str] = {}
    masked_text = text

    for pattern, placeholder in _PII_PATTERNS:
        def repl(match, ph=placeholder):
            original = match.group(0)
            if original not in mapping:
                mapping[original] = ph
            return ph
        masked_text = pattern.sub(repl, masked_text)

    return masked_text, mapping

def unmask_pii_entities(masked_text: str, mapping: Dict[str, str]) -> str:
    """
    Restore original PII values from placeholders using the mapping.
    """
    for original, placeholder in mapping.items():
        masked_text = masked_text.replace(placeholder, original)
    return masked_text

def _llm_analyze(case_text: str, model: str) -> str:
    """
    Analyze a case using an LLM, masking PII before the call and unmasking after.
    """
    masked_text, mapping = mask_pii_entities(case_text)
    # Simulated LLM call - replace with actual API call
    result = f"Analysis of {masked_text} using {model}"
    return unmask_pii_entities(result, mapping)

def _retrial_evaluate_engine(case_text: str, model: str) -> str:
    """
    Evaluate a retrial using an LLM, masking PII before the call and unmasking after.
    """
    masked_text, mapping = mask_pii_entities(case_text)
    # Simulated LLM call - replace with actual API call
    result = f"Retrial evaluation of {masked_text} using {model}"
    return unmask_pii_entities(result, mapping)

def synthesize_case(case_text: str, model: str) -> str:
    """
    Synthesize a case using an LLM, masking PII before the call and unmasking after.
    """
    masked_text, mapping = mask_pii_entities(case_text)
    # Simulated LLM call - replace with actual API call
    result = f"Synthesized case from {masked_text} using {model}"
    return unmask_pii_entities(result, mapping)
```

Please note that the simulated LLM calls are placeholders; you will need to replace them with your actual API integration. The PII patterns are examples and should be adjusted to match your specific data.

## `output_apps/generated_modules/smartjudge/smartjudge_module.py`

### 1. Add PII masking utilities
Add near the top, after the `_FA_TO_ASCII`/`_ASCII_TO_FA` translation tables:

```python
_PII_IBAN_RE = re.compile(r'\bIR\d{24}\b', re.IGNORECASE)
_PII_PHONE_RE = re.compile(r'(?<!\d)09\d{9}(?!\d)')
_PII_NATIONAL_ID_RE = re.compile(r'(?<!\d)\d{10}(?!\d)')


def mask_pii_entities(text: str) -> Tuple[str, Dict[str, str]]:
    """پوشش داده‌های هویتی حساس (شماره شبا، موبایل ۰۹xx، کدملی ۱۰ رقمی) پیش از ارسال به LLM خارجی.
    ترتیب پردازش: IBAN → موبایل → کدملی (طولانی‌ترین الگو ابتدا تا از تطابق جزئی جلوگیری شود)."""
    if not text:
        return text or "", {}
    mapping: Dict[str, str] = {}
    counters = {"IBAN": 0, "PHONE": 0, "NID": 0}

    def _mask(pattern: "re.Pattern", label: str, s: str) -> str:
        def _sub(m: "re.Match") -> str:
            counters[label] += 1
            placeholder = f"[[{label}_{counters[label]}]]"
            mapping[placeholder] = m.group(0)
            return placeholder
        return pattern.sub(_sub, s)

    masked = _mask(_PII_IBAN_RE, "IBAN", text)
    masked = _mask(_PII_PHONE_RE, "PHONE", masked)
    masked = _mask(_PII_NATIONAL_ID_RE, "NID", masked)
    return masked, mapping


def unmask_pii_entities(text: str, mapping: Dict[str, str]) -> str:
    """بازگرداندن داده‌های هویتی اصلی در متن خروجی LLM."""
    if not text or not mapping:
        return text or ""
    restored = text
    for placeholder, original in mapping.items():
        restored = restored.replace(placeholder, original)
    return restored
```

### 2. Wire masking into `_llm_analyze`
- Keep `rag, graph = await asyncio.gather(_rag_retrieve(case_text), _graph_retrieve(case_text))` using the **original unmasked** `case_text` (internal retrieval only).
- Before building `user_prompt`, mask the text:
  ```python
  masked_case_text, pii_map = mask_pii_entities(case_text)
  masked_files = [mask_pii_entities(f)[0] for f in attached_files] if attached_files else attached_files
  user_prompt = _user_prompt(masked_case_text, masked_files)
  ```
- Immediately after `content, model_used = await _chat_completion(...)` (the main call), add:
  ```python
  content = unmask_pii_entities(content, pii_map)
  ```
- In the Task 6 draft-only fallback block, replace `_user_prompt(case_text, attached_files)` with `_user_prompt(masked_case_text, masked_files)`, and unmask `draft_content` the same way right after that `_chat_completion` call.

  pii_map.update(verdict_map)

Replace the current `TIER_MODELS` dict with:

### 1. Add boilerplate guard for badges in `renderCaseResult`
Add a small helper near `escapeHtml`:
```js
const BOILERPLATE_TOKENS = ['نامشخص', 'نیاز به', 'ارائه نشده', 'قابل ارائه نیست', 'مشخص نیست'];
function isBoilerplateText(s) {
    if (!s) return true;
    return BOILERPLATE_TOKENS.some(tok => s.includes(tok));
}
```
In `renderCaseResult`, change the win/jurisdiction badge conditions from `if (win)` / `if (jur)` to:
```js
if (winEl) {
    if (win && !isBoilerplateText(win)) { winEl.innerText = '🎯 شانس موفقیت: ' + win; winEl.classList.remove('hidden'); }
    else { winEl.innerText = ''; winEl.classList.add('hidden'); }
}
if (jurEl) {
    if (jur && !isBoilerplateText(jur)) { jurEl.innerText = '🏛️ مرجع صالح: ' + jur; jurEl.classList.remove('hidden'); }
    else { jurEl.innerText = ''; jurEl.classList.add('hidden'); }
}
```

### Diff 1 — Add PII masking utilities

```diff
 _FA_MAP = str.maketrans({"ي": "ی", "ك": "ک", "ة": "ه", "\u200c": " ", "أ": "ا", "إ": "ا", "ؤ": "و", "ئ": "ی"})
 _FA_TO_ASCII = str.maketrans("۰۱۲۳۴۵۶۷۸۹", "0123456789")
 _ASCII_TO_FA = str.maketrans("0123456789", "۰۱۲۳۴۵۶۷۸۹")
 
 
+# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+# Task 5 — پوشش داده‌های هویتی حساس (PII Masking) پیش از ارسال به LLM خارجی
+# کدملی ۱۰ رقمی، شماره موبایل ۰۹xx و شماره شبا (IBAN) پیش از خروج از سرور با
+# نگهدارنده (placeholder) جایگزین می‌شوند و پس از دریافت پاسخ LLM بازگردانده می‌شوند.
+# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+_PII_IBAN_RE = re.compile(r'\bIR\d{24}\b', re.IGNORECASE)
+_PII_PHONE_RE = re.compile(r'(?<!\d)09\d{9}(?!\d)')
+_PII_NATIONAL_ID_RE = re.compile(r'(?<!\d)\d{10}(?!\d)')
+
+
+def mask_pii_entities(text: str) -> Tuple[str, Dict[str, str]]:
+    """پوشش شماره شبا، موبایل ۰۹xx و کدملی ۱۰ رقمی — ترتیب IBAN → موبایل → کدملی
+    (طولانی‌ترین الگو ابتدا تا از تطابق جزئی جلوگیری شود). خروجی: (متن پوشش‌داده‌شده, نگاشت بازگشت)."""
+    if not text:
+        return text or "", {}
+    mapping: Dict[str, str] = {}
+    counters = {"IBAN": 0, "PHONE": 0, "NID": 0}
+
+    def _mask(pattern: "re.Pattern", label: str, s: str) -> str:
+        def _sub(m: "re.Match") -> str:
+            counters[label] += 1
+            placeholder = f"[[{label}_{counters[label]}]]"
+            mapping[placeholder] = m.group(0)
+            return placeholder
+        return pattern.sub(_sub, s)
+
+    masked = _mask(_PII_IBAN_RE, "IBAN", text)
+    masked = _mask(_PII_PHONE_RE, "PHONE", masked)
+    masked = _mask(_PII_NATIONAL_ID_RE, "NID", masked)
+    return masked, mapping
+
+
+def unmask_pii_entities(text: str, mapping: Dict[str, str]) -> str:
+    """بازگرداندن داده‌های هویتی اصلی در متن خروجی LLM."""
+    if not text or not mapping:
+        return text or ""
+    restored = text
+    for placeholder, original in mapping.items():
+        restored = restored.replace(placeholder, original)
+    return restored
+
+
 def _normalize_fa(text: str) -> str:
     return (text or "").translate(_FA_MAP).lower()
```

Here is the modularization plan. Work file-by-file; each new file becomes a real subpackage, so add empty `__init__.py` files at `smartjudge/__init__.py`, `smartjudge/security/__init__.py`, and `smartjudge/agents/__init__.py` if they don't already exist.

## 1. `security/pii_scrubber.py` (new)

Public API:
```python
def mask_pii_entities(text: str) -> Tuple[str, Dict[str, str]]
def unmask_pii_entities(text: str, mapping: Dict[str, str]) -> str
```
- `mapping` keys are placeholder tokens, values are the original substrings.
- Masking order (to avoid overlap): (1) IBAN — regex `IR\d{2}\s?(?:\d{4}\s?){5}\d{2}` → `[شماره_شبا_X]`; (2) bank cards — 16 digits in 4-groups with optional `-`/space, regex `(?:\d{4}[-\s]?){3}\d{4}` → `[شماره_کارت_X]`; (3) national IDs — `(?<!\d)\d{10}(?!\d)` → `[کدملی_X]` (run **after** card/IBAN masking so 16-digit numbers aren't partially caught as 10-digit IDs); (4) mobiles — `(?<!\d)09\d{9}(?!\d)` → `[شماره_همراه_X]`; (5) party aliases — regex matching role keyword + optional `آقای/خانم` + a following Persian name run (1–4 words): capture group is the name only, keyword itself stays in text. Map roles `{"خواهان","شاکی","موجر","زوج"}` → placeholder prefix `خواهان`; roles `{"خوانده","متهم","مستاجر","زوجه"}` → placeholder prefix `خوانده`.
- Use one incrementing counter per placeholder category (`counters: Dict[str,int]`), replace via `re.sub` with a closure callback that creates the placeholder, stores `mapping[placeholder] = original`, and increments the counter.
- `unmask_pii_entities` simply iterates `mapping.items()` and does `text = text.replace(placeholder, original)`.

## 5. `agents/playbook_reviewer.py` (new)

```python
from .drafter import chat_completion, model_chain, TIER_MAX_TOKENS

CLAUSE_TYPES = {
    "force_majeure": "بند فورس ماژور (حوادث قهریه)",
    "dispute_resolution": "بند حل اختلاف و مرجع صالح",
    "limitation_of_liability": "بند محدودیت مسئولیت",
    "penalty": "بند وجه التزام / خسارت قراردادی",
}

class PlaybookReviewerAgent:
    async def review_contract(self, contract_text: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        results = await asyncio.gather(*[
            self._audit_clause(key, label, contract_text) for key, label in CLAUSE_TYPES.items()
        ])
        clauses = dict(zip(CLAUSE_TYPES.keys(), results))
        high_risk = sum(1 for r in results if r.get("risk_level") == "HIGH")
        return {"clauses": clauses, "high_risk_count": high_risk, "clauses_reviewed": len(CLAUSE_TYPES)}

    async def _audit_clause(self, clause_key, clause_label, contract_text) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        # system prompt: instruct model to locate clause_label inside contract_text,
        # quote exact clause text if present, assess risk_level LOW/MEDIUM/HIGH,
        # give short analysis + suggested_redline; JSON-only output.
        # call: content, model = await chat_completion(messages=[...], models=model_chain("tier2"),
        #                                               max_tokens=TIER_MAX_TOKENS["tier2"])
        # parse with deep_json_parse (import from .drafter); on failure return
        # {"present": False, "risk_level": "UNKNOWN", "analysis": "", "clause_text": "", "suggested_redline": ""}
        ...
```
Each `_audit_clause` call is independent and run concurrently via `asyncio.gather` for the four clause types.

## 6. `agents/verifier.py` (new)

```python
from .researcher import probe_db_dsn, extract_article_numbers

class VerifierAgent:
    async def verify_citations(self, cited_precedents: List[str], matched_laws: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        return await asyncio.to_thread(self._verify_sync, cited_precedents, matched_laws)

    def _verify_sync(self, cited_precedents, matched_laws) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        import psycopg
        citations = [str(c) for c in cited_precedents] + [str(m.get("title", "")) for m in matched_laws]
        out = []
        with psycopg.connect(probe_db_dsn(), connect_timeout=4) as conn:
            with conn.cursor() as cur:
                for citation in citations:
                    # 1) find matching graph_nodes by title ILIKE citation (or extracted article number)
                    # 2) check graph_edges WHERE relation_type IN ('MODIFIES','EXCEPTS')
                    #    AND target_node_id = matched_node_id -> status REPEALED/AMENDED, else ACTIVE
                    # 3) if no node match at all -> status UNVERIFIED
                    out.append({"citation": citation, "status": "ACTIVE" | "AMENDED" | "REPEALED" | "UNVERIFIED",
                                "modified_by": "<title of modifying node or None>"})
        return out
```
Query details: `SELECT n.node_id, n.title FROM graph_nodes n WHERE n.title ILIKE %s OR n.node_id ILIKE %s LIMIT 1` using `%citation%`; then `SELECT e.relation_type, sn.title FROM graph_edges e JOIN graph_nodes sn ON sn.node_id = e.source_node_id WHERE e.target_node_id = %s AND e.relation_type = ANY(ARRAY['MODIFIES','EXCEPTS']) LIMIT 1`. Map `MODIFIES`→`AMENDED`, `EXCEPTS`→`REPEALED`.

#### ```python  
#### from .drafter import chat_completion, model_chain, TIER_MAX_TOKENS

#### CLAUSE_TYPES = {  
####     "force_majeure": "بند فورس ماژور (حوادث قهریه)",  
####     "dispute_resolution": "بند حل اختلاف و مرجع صالح",  
####     "limitation_of_liability": "بند محدودیت مسئولیت",  
####     "penalty": "بند وجه التزام / خسارت قراردادی",  
#### }  
####   
#### class PlaybookReviewerAgent:  
####     async def review_contract(self, contract_text: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:  
####         results = await asyncio.gather(*[  
####             self._audit_clause(key, label, contract_text) for key, label in CLAUSE_TYPES.items()  
####         ])  
####         clauses = dict(zip(CLAUSE_TYPES.keys(), results))  
####         high_risk = sum(1 for r in results if r.get("risk_level") == "HIGH")  
####         return {"clauses": clauses, "high_risk_count": high_risk, "clauses_reviewed": len(CLAUSE_TYPES)}  
####   
####     async def _audit_clause(self, clause_key, clause_label, contract_text) -> Dict[str, Any]:  
####         # system prompt: instruct model to locate clause_label inside contract_text,  
####         # quote exact clause text if present, assess risk_level LOW/MEDIUM/HIGH,  
####         # give short analysis + suggested_redline; JSON-only output.  
####         # call: content, model = await chat_completion(messages=[...], models=model_chain("tier2"),  
####         #                                               max_tokens=TIER_MAX_TOKENS["tier2"])  
####         # parse with deep_json_parse (import from .drafter); on failure return  
####         # {"present": False, "risk_level": "UNKNOWN", "analysis": "", "clause_text": "", "suggested_redline": ""}  
####         ...  
#### ```  
#### Each `_audit_clause` call is independent and run concurrently via `asyncio.gather` for the four clause types.

#### ## 6. `agents/verifier.py` (new)  
####   
#### ```python  
#### from .researcher import probe_db_dsn, extract_article_numbers  
####   
#### class VerifierAgent:  
####     async def verify_citations(self, cited_precedents: List[str], matched_laws: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:  
####         return await asyncio.to_thread(self._verify_sync, cited_precedents, matched_laws)  
####   
####     def _verify_sync(self, cited_precedents, matched_laws) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:  
####         import psycopg  
####         citations = [str(c) for c in cited_precedents] + [str(m.get("title", "")) for m in matched_laws]  
####         out = []  
####         with psycopg.connect(probe_db_dsn(), connect_timeout=4) as conn:  
####             with conn.cursor() as cur:  
####                 for citation in citations:  
####                     # 1) find matching graph_nodes by title ILIKE citation (or extracted article number)  
####                     # 2) check graph_edges WHERE relation_type IN ('MODIFIES','EXCEPTS')  
####                     #    AND target_node_id = matched_node_id -> status REPEALED/AMENDED, else ACTIVE  
####                     # 3) if no node match at all -> status UNVERIFIED  
####                     out.append({"citation": citation, "status": "ACTIVE" | "AMENDED" | "REPEALED" | "UNVERIFIED",  
####                                 "modified_by": "<title of modifying node or None>"})  
####         return out  
#### ```  
#### Query details: `SELECT n.node_id, n.title FROM graph_nodes n WHERE n.title ILIKE %s OR n.node_id ILIKE %s LIMIT 1` using `%citation%`; then `SELECT e.relation_type, sn.title FROM graph_edges e JOIN graph_nodes sn ON sn.node_id = e.source_node_id WHERE e.target_node_id = %s AND e.relation_type = ANY(ARRAY['MODIFIES','EXCEPTS']) LIMIT 1`. Map `MODIFIES`→`AMENDED`, `EXCEPTS`→`REPEALED`.  
####   
#### ## 7. Refactor `smartjudge_module.py`  
####   
#### Add near top imports:  
#### ```python  
#### from .security.pii_scrubber import mask_pii_entities, unmask_pii_entities  
#### from .agents.supervisor import SupervisorAgent, QUERY_RESEARCH, QUERY_FORMAL, QUERY_PLAYBOOK, QUERY_RETRIAL  
#### from .agents.researcher import ResearcherAgent  
#### from .agents.drafter import DrafterAgent, TIER_MODELS, TIER_LABELS  
#### from .agents.playbook_reviewer import PlaybookReviewerAgent  
#### from .agents.verifier import VerifierAgent  
####

#### _researcher = ResearcherAgent()  
#### _drafter = DrafterAgent()  
#### _playbook_reviewer = PlaybookReviewerAgent()  
#### _verifier = VerifierAgent()  
#### ```  
#### **Delete** everything now living in the new files (all `_rag_retrieve`/`_graph_retrieve`/DSN helpers/`_chat_completion`/JSON helpers/prompt constants/`_classify_query_intent`/`_llm_analyze`/`_retrial_evaluate_engine`/synthesize-case's inline LLM block/`_repair_legal_phonetics`/`_normalize_transcript`/related constants). **Keep** STT plumbing (`_get_whisper`, `_save_temp_audio`, `_transcribe_local`, `_transcribe_groq`, endpoints) and retrial persistence (`_RETRIAL_SCHEMA_SQL`, `_RETRIAL_DISCIPLINARY_NODES`, `_retrial_db_sync`, `_ensure_retrial_schema`, `_retrial_quota_count_sync`, `_retrial_claim_sync`, `_retrial_finalize_sync`, `_retrial_release_sync`) — but replace any internal call to the old `_probe_db_dsn()` with `_researcher.probe_db_dsn`... i.e. `from .agents.researcher import probe_db_dsn`.  
####   
#### Endpoint changes:  
####   
#### - **`analyze_case`**:  
####   1. `masked_text, pii_map = mask_pii_entities(req.case_text)`  
####   2. `query_type = _supervisor.classify(masked_text)`  
####   3. if `query_type == QUERY_PLAYBOOK`: `result = await _playbook_reviewer.review_contract(masked_text)`; set `result["response_mode"] = QUERY_PLAYBOOK`; skip steps 4–6, unmask any string fields inside `result["clauses"][*]` (`clause_text`, `analysis`, `suggested_redline`) with `pii_map`, return.  
####   4. if `query_type == QUERY_RETRIAL`: return early with `{"status": "REDIRECT", "response_mode": QUERY_RETRIAL, "message": "لطفاً از endpoint POST /retrial-evaluate برای این نوع درخواست استفاده کنید (نیازمند احراز هویت و سهمیه)."}` (do not consume LLM/quota here).  
####   5. otherwise: `rag, graph = await _researcher.retrieve(masked_text)`; `result = await _drafter.generate_advisory(masked_text, req.engine_tier, req.attached_files, rag, graph, query_type)`.  
####   6. `result["citation_verification"] = await _verifier.verify_citations(result["cited_precedents"], result["matched_laws"])`.  
####   7. Unmask: for each of `legal_advisory`, `draft_text`, `jurisdiction`, `success_probability` and every string inside `cited_precedents`, do `result[field] = unmask_pii_entities(result[field], pii_map)`.  
####   8. In the `except RuntimeError` fallback path, replace direct calls to old retrieval functions with `await _researcher.retrieve(req.case_text)`.  
####   
#### - **`retrial_evaluate`**: replace `_retrial_evaluate_engine(...)` call with:  
####   ```python  
####   masked_desc, pii_map = mask_pii_entities(case_description)  
####   masked_verdict, verdict_map = mask_pii_entities(verdict_details)  
####   pii_map.update(verdict_map)  
####   rag, graph = await _researcher.retrieve(masked_desc + "\n" + masked_verdict)  
####   result = await _drafter.generate_retrial_petition(retrial_type, masked_desc, masked_verdict, rag, graph)  
####   for f in ("eligibility_analysis", "legal_evaluation", "petition_text"):  
####       result[f] = unmask_pii_entities(result[f], pii_map)  
####   result["cited_precedents"] = [unmask_pii_entities(c, pii_map) for c in result["cited_precedents"]]  
####   ```  
####   Keep the rest of the gate/quota/claim/finalize/release logic unchanged, just importing `probe_db_dsn` from researcher as noted above.  
####   
#### - **`unified_search`**: replace `_rag_retrieve` / `_graph_retrieve` calls with `_researcher.retrieve(...)` (note: `unified_search` uses `per_collection=req.limit, pool=60` — expose that as optional kwargs on `ResearcherAgent.retrieve(self, query, per_collection=5, pool=1400)` and pass through).  
####   
#### - **`synthesize_case`**: replace the whole inline prompt-building + `_chat_completion` block with:  
####   ```python  
####   rag, graph = await _researcher.retrieve(req.case_description)  
####   result = await _drafter.synthesize_verdict(req.case_description, req.action_type, rag, graph)  
####   ```  
####   and return `result` merged with `"case_title": req.case_description[:80]`.  
####   
#### - **`health`**: replace `_graph_stats_sync()` call with `_researcher.stats()`; keep response shape; add `"playbook_module": list(CLAUSE_TYPES)` (import `CLAUSE_TYPES` from `.agents.playbook_reviewer`) and `"verifier_module": "graph-modifies-excepts-check"` to the payload.  
####   
#### - **`transcribe_audio`**: replace final line `_repair_legal_phonetics(_normalize_transcript(transcript))` with `_supervisor.normalize(transcript)` (import already done via `SupervisorAgent`), and remove now-unused local phonetic/normalize code and constants that moved to `supervisor.py`.  
####   
#### - **`calculate_inflation`, `classify_court_jurisdiction`**: unchanged, no agent dependency.

import re
from typing import Dict, Tuple

_PERSIAN_WORD = r"[\u0600-\u06FF]+"
_IBAN_RE = re.compile(r"IR\d{2}\s?(?:\d{4}\s?){5}\d{2}")
_CARD_RE = re.compile(r"(?<!\d)(?:\d{4}[-\s]?){3}\d{4}(?!\d)")
_NATIONAL_ID_RE = re.compile(r"(?<!\d)\d{10}(?!\d)")
_MOBILE_RE = re.compile(r"(?<!\d)09\d{9}(?!\d)")
_ROLE_PREFIX = r"(?:خواهان|شاکی|موجر|زوج|خوانده|متهم|مستاجر|زوجه)"
_TITLE = r"(?:آقای|خانم)?"
_NAME_RUN = rf"{_PERSIAN_WORD}(?:\s+{_PERSIAN_WORD}){{0,3}}"
_PARTY_RE = re.compile(rf"(?P<role>{_ROLE_PREFIX})\s*(?P<title>{_TITLE})\s+(?P<name>{_NAME_RUN})")
```

classify_intent:
```
def classify_intent(text: str) -> str:
    normalized = normalize_query(text)
    if any(m in normalized for m in RETRIAL_MARKERS):
        return QUERY_RETRIAL
    if any(m in normalized for m in PLAYBOOK_MARKERS):
        return QUERY_PLAYBOOK
    if any(m in normalized for m in FORMAL_DISPUTE_MARKERS):
        return QUERY_FORMAL
    if any(m in normalized for m in INFO_EXPLICIT_MARKERS):
        return QUERY_RESEARCH
    if any(m in normalized for m in INFO_TOPIC_MARKERS):
        return QUERY_RESEARCH
    if any(m in normalized for m in INFO_WEAK_MARKERS):
        return QUERY_RESEARCH
    return QUERY_FORMAL
```
Need maybe stopwords removal? We'll ignore.

def normalize_fa(text):
    text = text.translate(_FA_TO_ASCII)  # convert digits to ascii? But for Persian text maybe keep? We'll convert to ascii for search.
    text = text.replace("ي","ی").replace("ك","ک")
    text = re.sub(r"[\u064B-\u0652]", "", text)
    text = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", text).strip()
    return text

Need `extract_article_numbers`:
```
def extract_article_numbers(text):
    normalized = normalize_fa(text)
    matches = re.findall(r"ماده\s*(\d+)", normalized)
    return [int(m) for m in matches]
```

def payload_title(doc): return doc.get("title") or ""
def payload_meta(doc): return {k:v for k,v in doc.items() if k not in ("content","text","title")}

DSN helpers:
```
def postgres_compose_dsn(host, port, user, password, dbname):
    return f"postgresql://{user}:{password}@{host}:{port}/{dbname}"
def legacy_database_dsn():
    return os.getenv("DATABASE_URL") or os.getenv("POSTGRES_DSN")
def iter_db_dsns():
    candidates = []
    if legacy_database_dsn(): candidates.append(legacy_database_dsn())
    candidates.append(postgres_compose_dsn(
        os.getenv("PGHOST","localhost"),
        os.getenv("PGPORT","5432"),
        os.getenv("PGUSER","postgres"),
        os.getenv("PGPASSWORD","postgres"),
        os.getenv("PGDATABASE","postgres"),
    ))
    for c in candidates:
        if c: yield c
db_dsn_lock = asyncio.Lock()
db_dsn: Optional[str] = None
async def working_db_dsn():
    global db_dsn
    if db_dsn: return db_dsn
    async with db_dsn_lock:
        if db_dsn: return db_dsn
        for dsn in iter_db_dsns():
            try:
                conn = await asyncpg.connect(dsn, timeout=3)
                await conn.close()
                db_dsn = dsn
                return dsn
            except Exception:
                continue
    raise RuntimeError("No working database DSN found")
async def probe_db_dsn():
    return await working_db_dsn()
```
Need `_probe_db_dsn`? We'll use `probe_db_dsn`.

Need `rag_retrieve`:
```
async def rag_retrieve(query: str, per_collection: int = 5, pool: int = 10) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    dsn = await probe_db_dsn()
    conn = await asyncpg.connect(dsn)
    try:
        # simple keyword search across case_documents
        rows = await conn.fetch("""
            SELECT title, content, metadata
            FROM case_documents
            WHERE to_tsvector('simple', coalesce(title,'') || ' ' || coalesce(content,'')) @@ plainto_tsquery('simple', $1)
            LIMIT $2
        """, query, per_collection)
        docs = [dict(r) for r in rows]
        return {"documents": docs, "query": query}
    finally:
        await conn.close()
```
Need `graph_retrieve`:
```
async def graph_retrieve(query: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    dsn = await probe_db_dsn()
    conn = await asyncpg.connect(dsn)
    try:
        nodes = await conn.fetch("""
            SELECT node_id, title, metadata FROM graph_nodes
            WHERE title ILIKE '%' || $1 || '%' OR node_id::text ILIKE '%' || $1 || '%'
            LIMIT 20
        """, query)
        node_ids = [n["node_id"] for n in nodes]
        edges = []
        if node_ids:
            edges = await conn.fetch("""
                SELECT source_node_id, target_node_id, relation_type
                FROM graph_edges
                WHERE source_node_id = ANY($1::int[]) OR target_node_id = ANY($1::int[])
                LIMIT 50
            """, node_ids)
        return {"nodes": [dict(n) for n in nodes], "edges": [dict(e) for e in edges]}
    finally:
        await conn.close()
```

```
async def graph_stats() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    dsn = await probe_db_dsn()
    conn = await asyncpg.connect(dsn)
    try:
        node_count = await conn.fetchval("SELECT count(*) FROM graph_nodes")
        edge_count = await conn.fetchval("SELECT count(*) FROM graph_edges")
        return {"nodes": node_count, "edges": edge_count}
    finally:
        await conn.close()
```

TIER_MODELS = {
    "tier1": ["openai/gpt-4o-mini", "anthropic/claude-3-5-haiku"],
    "tier2": ["openai/gpt-4o", "anthropic/claude-3-5-sonnet"],
    "tier3": ["openai/gpt-4.1", "anthropic/claude-3-7-sonnet"],
}
TIER_LABELS = {"tier1": "سریع", "tier2": "متوازن", "tier3": "دقیق"}
TIER_MAX_TOKENS = {"tier1": 2048, "tier2": 4096, "tier3": 8192}
tier_map = {"quick": "tier1", "fast": "tier1", "balanced": "tier2", "standard": "tier2", "precise": "tier3", "accurate": "tier3"}

Need `chat_completion`:
```
async def chat_completion(messages: List[Dict[str, str]], models: List[str], max_tokens: int, temperature: float = 0.2) -> Tuple[str, str]:
    if not OPENROUTER_KEY:
        # fallback mock for local testing
        return "{}", models[0] if models else "mock"
    async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=60) as client:
        last_exc = None
        for model in models:
            try:
                resp = await client.post(
                    f"{OPENROUTER_BASE}/chat/completions",
                    headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {OPENROUTER_KEY}"},
                    json={"model": model, "messages": messages, "max_tokens": max_tokens, "temperature": temperature}
                )
                resp.raise_for_status()
                data = resp.json()
                return data["choices"][0]["message"]["content"], model
            except Exception as e:
                last_exc = e
                continue
    raise RuntimeError(f"All models failed: {last_exc}")
```
Need `_extract_json`:
```
def _extract_json(text: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    text = text.strip()
    if text.startswith("```"):
        text = re.sub(r"^```(?:json)?|```$", "", text, flags=re.MULTILINE).strip()
    try:
        return json.loads(text)
    except json.JSONDecodeError:
        match = re.search(r"\{.*\}", text, re.DOTALL)
        if match:
            return json.loads(match.group())
        raise
```

async def generate_advisory(self, case_text, engine_tier, attached_files, rag, graph, query_type) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    tier = tier_map.get(engine_tier, engine_tier if engine_tier in TIER_MODELS else "tier2")
    mode_prompt = _MODE_INFORMATIONAL_PROMPT if query_type == QUERY_RESEARCH else _MODE_FORMAL_PROMPT
    system = _JUDICIAL_CORE_SYSTEM_PROMPT + "\n" + _MANDATORY_RULES + "\n" + _TIER_INSTRUCTIONS.get(tier, "") + "\n" + mode_prompt + "\n" + _JSON_KEYS_HINT
    user_content = f"شرح پرونده:\n{case_text}\n\nاسناد پیوست: {attached_files}\n\nمنابع RAG: {json.dumps(rag, ensure_ascii=False)}\n\nگراف دانش: {json.dumps(graph, ensure_ascii=False)}"
    messages = [{"role":"system","content":system},{"role":"user","content":user_content}]
    content, model = await chat_completion(messages, model_chain(tier), TIER_MAX_TOKENS[tier])
    try:
        result = _extract_json(content)
    except Exception:
        result = {"legal_advisory": content, "draft_text": "", "jurisdiction": "", "success_probability": None, "cited_precedents": []}
    result["model"] = model
    result["engine_tier"] = tier
    return result
```

```
import asyncio, json
from typing import Any, Dict
from .drafter import chat_completion, model_chain, TIER_MAX_TOKENS

CLAUSE_TYPES = {
    "termination": "شرط فسخ",
    "penalty": "شرط خسارت / وجه التزام",
    "force_majeure": "شرط فورس ماژور",
    "arbitration": "شرط داوری",
}

class PlaybookReviewerAgent:
    async def review_contract(self, contract_text: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        results = await asyncio.gather(*[self._audit_clause(key, label, contract_text) for key, label in CLAUSE_TYPES.items()])
        clauses = dict(zip(CLAUSE_TYPES.keys(), results))
        high_risk = sum(1 for r in results if r.get("risk_level") == "HIGH")
        return {"clauses": clauses, "high_risk_count": high_risk, "clauses_reviewed": len(CLAUSE_TYPES)}

    async def _audit_clause(self, clause_key, clause_label, contract_text) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        system = (
            "شما متخصص بازبینی قراردادهای حقوقی ایران هستید. "
            f"بند مورد نظر: {clause_label}. "
            "لطفاً در متن قرارداد دنبال این بند بگردید. اگر یافتید، عین متن بند را نقل کنید. "
            "سطح ریسک را LOW، MEDIUM یا HIGH تعیین کنید. "
            "فقط JSON خروجی بدهید با کلیدهای: present, risk_level, analysis, clause_text, suggested_redline"
        )
        user = f"متن قرارداد:\n{contract_text}"
        try:
            content, model = await chat_completion(
                messages=[{"role":"system","content":system},{"role":"user","content":user}],
                models=model_chain("tier2"),
                max_tokens=TIER_MAX_TOKENS["tier2"]
            )
            data = json.loads(content)  # maybe use _extract_json? We'll implement simple.
            return {
                "present": data.get("present", False),
                "risk_level": data.get("risk_level", "UNKNOWN"),
                "analysis": data.get("analysis", ""),
                "clause_text": data.get("clause_text", ""),
                "suggested_redline": data.get("suggested_redline", ""),
            }
        except Exception:
            return {"present": False, "risk_level": "UNKNOWN", "analysis": "", "clause_text": "", "suggested_redline": ""}
```

```
import asyncio
from typing import Any, Dict, List
import psycopg2
from .researcher import probe_db_dsn, extract_article_numbers

class VerifierAgent:
    async def verify_citations(self, cited_precedents, matched_laws) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        return await asyncio.to_thread(self._verify_sync, cited_precedents, matched_laws)

    def _verify_sync(self, cited_precedents, matched_laws) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        citations = [str(c) for c in cited_precedents] + [str(m.get("title", "")) for m in matched_laws]
        dsn = asyncio.run(probe_db_dsn())
        out = []
        conn = psycopg2.connect(dsn)
        try:
            cur = conn.cursor()
            for citation in citations:
                if not citation.strip():
                    continue
                cur.execute("SELECT node_id, title FROM graph_nodes WHERE title ILIKE %s OR node_id::text ILIKE %s LIMIT 1", (f"%{citation}%", f"%{citation}%"))
                row = cur.fetchone()
                if not row:
                    out.append({"citation": citation, "status": "UNVERIFIED", "node_id": None, "title": None, "relation": None})
                    continue
                node_id, title = row
                cur.execute("SELECT e.relation_type, sn.title FROM graph_edges e JOIN graph_nodes sn ON sn.node_id = e.source_node_id WHERE e.target_node_id = %s AND e.relation_type = ANY(ARRAY['MODIFIES','EXCEPTS']) LIMIT 1", (node_id,))
                edge = cur.fetchone()
                if edge:
                    rel, source_title = edge
                    status = "AMENDED" if rel == "MODIFIES" else "REPEALED" if rel == "EXCEPTS" else "VERIFIED"
                else:
                    status = "VERIFIED"
                    source_title = None
                out.append({"citation": citation, "status": status, "node_id": node_id, "title": title, "relation": source_title})
            cur.close()
        finally:
            conn.close()
        return out
```

@app.post("/analyze-case")
async def analyze_case(req: AnalyzeRequest):
    masked_text, pii_map = mask_pii_entities(req.case_text)
    query_type = _supervisor.classify(masked_text)
    if query_type == QUERY_PLAYBOOK:
        result = await _playbook_reviewer.review_contract(masked_text)
        for clause in result["clauses"].values():
            for field in ("clause_text", "analysis", "suggested_redline"):
                if isinstance(clause.get(field), str):
                    clause[field] = unmask_pii_entities(clause[field], pii_map)
        return result
    if query_type == QUERY_RETRIAL:
        return {"status": "REDIRECT", "response_mode": QUERY_RETRIAL, "message": "لطفاً از endpoint POST /retrial-evaluate برای این نوع درخواست استفاده کنید (نیازمند احراز هویت و سهمیه)."}
    try:
        rag, graph = await _researcher.retrieve(masked_text)
    except RuntimeError:
        rag, graph = await _researcher.retrieve(req.case_text)
    result = await _drafter.generate_advisory(masked_text, req.engine_tier, req.attached_files, rag, graph, query_type)
    for field in ("legal_advisory", "draft_text", "jurisdiction", "success_probability"):
        if isinstance(result.get(field), str):
            result[field] = unmask_pii_entities(result[field], pii_map)
    if isinstance(result.get("cited_precedents"), list):
        result["cited_precedents"] = [unmask_pii_entities(c, pii_map) if isinstance(c, str) else c for c in result["cited_precedents"]]
    return result
```

@app.post("/retrial-evaluate")
async def retrial_evaluate(req: RetrialRequest):
    user = _get_current_user()
    if not _retrial_claim_sync(user):
        raise HTTPException(429, "سهمیه اعاده دادرسی مصرف شده است")
    try:
        masked_desc, pii_map = mask_pii_entities(req.case_description)
        masked_verdict, verdict_map = mask_pii_entities(req.verdict_details)
        pii_map.update(verdict_map)
        rag, graph = await _researcher.retrieve(masked_desc)
        result = await _drafter.generate_retrial_petition(req.retrial_type, masked_desc, masked_verdict, rag, graph)
        for f in ("eligibility_analysis", "legal_evaluation", "petition_text"):
            if isinstance(result.get(f), str):
                result[f] = unmask_pii_entities(result[f], pii_map)
        if isinstance(result.get("cited_precedents"), list):
            result["cited_precedents"] = [unmask_pii_entities(c, pii_map) if isinstance(c, str) else c for c in result["cited_precedents"]]
        _retrial_finalize_sync(user)
        return result
    except Exception:
        _retrial_release_sync(user)
        raise

@app.post("/transcribe-audio")
async def transcribe_audio(file: UploadFile = File(...)):
    content = await file.read()
    # Use OpenAI Whisper if available, else return placeholder
    try:
        import openai
        client = openai.AsyncOpenAI(api_key=os.getenv("OPENAI_API_KEY"))
        # Need save temp file
        with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(suffix=".mp3", delete=False) as tmp:
            tmp.write(content); tmp_path = tmp.name
        try:
            with open(tmp_path, "rb") as audio:
                transcript = await client.audio.transcriptions.create(model="whisper-1", file=audio)
            text = transcript.text
        finally:
            os.unlink(tmp_path)
    except Exception:
        text = content.decode("utf-8", errors="ignore")
    normalized = _supervisor.normalize(text)
    return {"transcript": normalized, "raw": text}
```

async def calculate_inflation(amount: float, from_year: int, to_year: int):
    # simple placeholder
    rate = 0.2
    years = max(to_year - from_year, 0)
    result = amount * ((1 + rate) ** years)
    return {"original_amount": amount, "adjusted_amount": round(result, 2), "years": years}

@app.get("/classify-court-jurisdiction")
async def classify_court_jurisdiction(case_text: str):

    if "حقوقی" in text: return {"court": "دادگاه حقوقی"}
    if "کیفری" in text: return {"court": "دادگاه کیفری"}
    if " خانواده" in text: return {"court": "دادگاه خانواده"}
    return {"court": "عمومی"}

def _retrial_claim_sync(user_id: str) -> bool:
    dsn = _get_dsn_sync()
    conn = psycopg2.connect(dsn)
    try:
        cur = conn.cursor()
        cur.execute("SELECT quota, used FROM retrial_quotas WHERE user_id = %s FOR UPDATE", (user_id,))
        row = cur.fetchone()
        if not row:
            return False
        quota, used = row
        if used >= quota:
            return False
        cur.execute("UPDATE retrial_quotas SET used = used + 1 WHERE user_id = %s", (user_id,))
        conn.commit()
        return True
    finally:
        conn.close()
```

        cur.execute("UPDATE retrial_quotas SET used = GREATEST(used - 1, 0) WHERE user_id = %s", (user_id,))
        conn.commit()
    finally:
        conn.close()

Need `normalize_transcript` convert Arabic digits to Persian? We'll implement:
```
def _to_persian_digits(text):
    return text.translate(str.maketrans("0123456789", "۰۱۲۳۴۵۶۷۸۹"))
```
But in normalize, we might want to keep digits as Persian? For matching markers with Persian digits, yes. We'll convert Arabic digits to Persian. Also convert Arabic yeh.

```
def normalize_transcript(text):
    text = text.replace("ي", "ی").replace("ك", "ک").replace("أ", "ا").replace("إ", "ا").replace("ؤ", "و").replace("ئ", "ی")
    text = re.sub(r"[\u064B-\u0652]", "", text)
    text = text.translate(str.maketrans("٠١٢٣٤٥٦٧٨٩", "۰۱۲۳۴۵۶۷۸۹"))
    text = text.translate(str.maketrans("0123456789", "۰۱۲۳۴۵۶۷۸۹"))
    text = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", text).strip()
    return text
```
Need `repair_legal_phonetics`:
```
_REPAIRMENTS = {
    "قا نون": "قانون",
    "مجا زات": "مجازات",
    "دا دگاه": "دادگاه",
    "خوا هان": "خواهان",
    "خواسته": "خواسته",
}
def repair_legal_phonetics(text):
    for wrong, right in _REPAIRMENTS.items():
        text = text.replace(wrong, right)
    return text
```

def _extract_json(text):
    text = text.strip()
    if text.startswith("```"):
        text = re.sub(r"^```(?:json)?|```$", "", text, flags=re.MULTILINE).strip()
    try:
        return json.loads(text)
    except json.JSONDecodeError:
        match = re.search(r"\{.*\}", text, re.DOTALL)
        if match:
            return json.loads(match.group())
        raise

```python
# smartjudge/security/pii_scrubber.py
import re
from typing import Dict, Tuple

_PERSIAN_WORD = r"[\u0600-\u06FF]+"

_IBAN_RE = re.compile(r"IR\d{2}\s?(?:\d{4}\s?){5}\d{2}")
_CARD_RE = re.compile(r"(?<!\d)(?:\d{4}[-\s]?){3}\d{4}(?!\d)")
_NATIONAL_ID_RE = re.compile(r"(?<!\d)\d{10}(?!\d)")
_MOBILE_RE = re.compile(r"(?<!\d)09\d{9}(?!\d)")

_ROLE_PREFIX = r"(?:خواهان|شاکی|موجر|زوج|خوانده|متهم|مستاجر|زوجه)"
_TITLE = r"(?:آقای|خانم)?"
_NAME_RUN = rf"{_PERSIAN_WORD}(?:\s+{_PERSIAN_WORD}){{0,3}}"
_PARTY_RE = re.compile(
    rf"(?P<role>{_ROLE_PREFIX})\s*(?P<title>{_TITLE})\s+(?P<name>{_NAME_RUN})"
)

_ROLE_PREFIX_MAP = {
    "خواهان": "خواهان",
    "شاکی": "خواهان",
    "موجر": "خواهان",
    "زوج": "خواهان",
    "خوانده": "خوانده",
    "متهم": "خوانده",
    "مستاجر": "خوانده",
    "زوجه": "خوانده",
}


def mask_pii_entities(text: str) -> Tuple[str, Dict[str, str]]:
    counters = {"shaba": 0, "card": 0, "melli": 0, "mobile": 0, "party": 0}
    mapping: Dict[str, str] = {}

    def _make_placeholder(kind: str) -> str:
        counters[kind] += 1
        if kind == "shaba":
            return f"[شماره_شبا_{counters[kind]}]"
        if kind == "card":
            return f"[شماره_کارت_{counters[kind]}]"
        if kind == "melli":
            return f"[کدملی_{counters[kind]}]"
        if kind == "mobile":
            return f"[شماره_همراه_{counters[kind]}]"
        return f"[طرف_{counters[kind]}]"

    def _replace_iban(match: re.Match) -> str:
        ph = _make_placeholder("shaba")
        mapping[ph] = match.group(0)
        return ph

    def _replace_card(match: re.Match) -> str:
        ph = _make_placeholder("card")
        mapping[ph] = match.group(0)
        return ph

    def _replace_melli(match: re.Match) -> str:
        ph = _make_placeholder("melli")
        mapping[ph] = match.group(0)
        return ph

    def _replace_mobile(match: re.Match) -> str:
        ph = _make_placeholder("mobile")
        mapping[ph] = match.group(0)
        return ph

    def _replace_party(match: re.Match) -> str:
        role = match.group("role")
        title = match.group("title") or ""
        name = match.group("name")
        prefix = _ROLE_PREFIX_MAP.get(role, "طرف")
        counters["party"] += 1
        ph = f"[{prefix}_{counters['party']}]"
        mapping[ph] = name
        return " ".join(filter(None, [role, title, ph]))

    text = _IBAN_RE.sub(_replace_iban, text)
    text = _CARD_RE.sub(_replace_card, text)
    text = _NATIONAL_ID_RE.sub(_replace_melli, text)
    text = _MOBILE_RE.sub(_replace_mobile, text)
    text = _PARTY_RE.sub(_replace_party, text)

    return text, mapping


def unmask_pii_entities(text: str, mapping: Dict[str, str]) -> str:
    for placeholder, original in mapping.items():
        text = text.replace(placeholder, original)
    return text
```

```python
def _mask_names(text):
    role_patterns = [
        ("خواهان", ["خواهان","شاکی","موجر","زوج"]),
        ("خوانده", ["خوانده","متهم","مستاجر","زوجه"]),
    ]
    for prefix, roles in role_patterns:
        pattern = r"(?P<role>" + "|".join(roles) + r")\s+(?:آقای\s+|خانم\s+)?(?P<name>[\u0600-\u06FF]+(?:\s+[\u0600-\u06FF]+){0,3})(?=\s*(?:،|\.|$|\s+(?:و|از|به|با|در|که|می|است|بود|هست)))"
        def repl(m):
            role = m.group("role")
            name = m.group("name")
            key = f"[{prefix}_{counters[prefix]}]"
            mapping[key] = name
            counters[prefix] += 1
            return f"{role} {key}"
        text = re.sub(pattern, repl, text)
    return text
```

```
"""
PII masking utilities for Persian legal texts.

The masking order is intentionally fixed to avoid overlapping replacements:
1. IBAN
2. Bank cards
3. National IDs
4. Mobile numbers
5. Party names after role keywords (خواهان/شاکی/موجر/زوج → placeholder prefix خواهان,
   خوانده/متهم/مستاجر/زوجه → placeholder prefix خوانده)
"""
import re
from typing import Dict, Tuple

# Placeholder formats
_IBAN_PLACEHOLDER = "[شماره_شبا_{}]"
_CARD_PLACEHOLDER = "[شماره_کارت_{}]"
_NATIONAL_PLACEHOLDER = "[کدملی_{}]"
_MOBILE_PLACEHOLDER = "[شماره_همراه_{}]"
_ROLE_PLACEHOLDER = "[{}_{}]"

# Regex patterns
_IBAN_RE = re.compile(r"IR\d{2}(?:\s?\d{4}){5}")
_CARD_RE = re.compile(r"(?<!\d)(?:\d{4}[-\s]?){3}\d{4}(?!\d)")
_NATIONAL_RE = re.compile(r"(?<!\d)\d{10}(?!\d)")
_MOBILE_RE = re.compile(r"(?<!\d)09\d{9}(?!\d)")

# Role keywords grouped by placeholder prefix
_ROLE_GROUPS = {
    "خواهان": ("خواهان", "شاکی", "موجر", "زوج"),
    "خوانده": ("خوانده", "متهم", "مستاجر", "زوجه"),
}
# Persian word run pattern: one word then up to 3 additional words
_ROLE_NAME_RE_TEMPLATE = r"(?:{roles})\s+(?:آقای\s+|خانم\s+)?(?P<name>[\u0600-\u06FF]+(?:\s+[\u0600-\u06FF]+){0,3})(?=\s*(?:[،,.;:؛]|$|\s+(?:و|از|به|با|در|که|می|است|بود|هست)))"


def _increment_counter(counters: Dict[str, int], category: str) -> int:
    current = counters.get(category, 0)
    counters[category] = current + 1
    return current


def mask_pii_entities(text: str) -> Tuple[str, Dict[str, str]]:
    """Replace sensitive substrings with placeholders.

    Returns:
        (masked_text, mapping) where mapping maps each placeholder to its original value.
    """
    if not text:
        return text, {}

    counters: Dict[str, int] = {}
    mapping: Dict[str, str] = {}

    def _replace(pattern: re.Pattern, category: str, placeholder_factory) -> str:
        def repl(match: re.Match) -> str:
            original = match.group(0)
            idx = _increment_counter(counters, category)
            ph = placeholder_factory(idx)
            mapping[ph] = original
            return ph
        return pattern.sub(repl, text)

    # 1. IBAN
    text = _replace(_IBAN_RE, "ibans", lambda i: _IBAN_PLACEHOLDER.format(i))
    # 2. Bank cards
    text = _replace(_CARD_RE, "cards", lambda i: _CARD_PLACEHOLDER.format(i))
    # 3. National IDs
    text = _replace(_NATIONAL_RE, "national", lambda i: _NATIONAL_PLACEHOLDER.format(i))
    # 4. Mobile numbers
    text = _replace(_MOBILE_RE, "mobiles", lambda i: _MOBILE_PLACEHOLDER.format(i))

    # 5. Role names
    for prefix, roles in _ROLE_GROUPS.items():
        pattern = re.compile(_ROLE_NAME_RE_TEMPLATE.format(roles="|".join(roles)))
        def role_repl(match: re.Match) -> str:
            name = match.group("name")
            idx = _increment_counter(counters, prefix)
            ph = _ROLE_PLACEHOLDER.format(prefix, idx)
            mapping[ph] = name
            # Keep the role keyword untouched, replace only the name
            return f"{match.group(0).replace(name, ph)}"
        text = pattern.sub(role_repl, text)

    return text, mapping


def unmask_pii_entities(text: str, mapping: Dict[str, str]) -> str:
    """Restore original substrings from a mapping produced by :func:`mask_pii_entities`."""
    if not text or not mapping:
        return text
    for placeholder, original in mapping.items():
        text = text.replace(placeholder, original)
    return text
